// =================================================================

// EXCEL PARSING - Dinamik danışman keşfi

// =================================================================

function isLikelyDanName(v){if(!v||typeof v!=='string')return false;const n=normTR(v);if(n.length<5||n.length>40)return false;if(/\d/.test(n))return false;const bad=['VOC','GENEL','MEMNUNIYET','DENEYIM','MUSTERI','DEGER','KALITE','PARA','ANKET','DONEM','OCAK','SUBAT','MART','NISAN','MAYIS','HAZIRAN','TEMMUZ','AGUSTOS','EYLUL','EKIM','KASIM','ARALIK','ANONIM','PUAN','ENDEKS','TOPLAM','ORAN'];if(bad.some(x=>n.includes(x)))return false;return n.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length>=2;}

 

function findNameRow(data){

  for(let r=9;r<18;r++){

    if(!data[r])continue;

    let count=0;

    for(let c=8;c<60;c++){

      const v=data[r][c];

      if(v&&typeof v==='string'){

        const t=v.trim();

        const u=t.toUpperCase();

        if(t.length>=3&&/\s/.test(t)&&!u.includes('PUAN')&&!u.includes('DÖNEM')&&!u.includes('ENDEKS')&&!u.includes('TOPLAM')&&!u.includes('ORAN'))count++;

      }

    }

    if(count>=2)return r;

  }

  return 12;

}

function findNameCols(data,nameRow){const cols=[];if(!data[nameRow])return cols;for(let c=0;c<data[nameRow].length;c++){const v=data[nameRow][c];if(!v||typeof v!=='string')continue;const t=v.trim();const u=t.toUpperCase();if(t.length<3||!/\s/.test(t))continue;if(u.includes('PUAN')||u.includes('DÖNEM')||u.includes('ENDEKS')||u.includes('TOPLAM')||u.includes('ORAN'))continue;cols.push(c);}return cols;}

 

function handleExcelSelect(inp){const f=inp.files[0];if(f)readExcelFile(f);}

function handleExcelDrop(e){e.preventDefault();e.currentTarget.classList.remove('drag');const f=e.dataTransfer.files[0];if(f&&(f.name.endsWith('.xlsx')||f.name.endsWith('.xls')))readExcelFile(f);}

function readExcelFile(file){const reader=new FileReader();reader.onload=e=>{try{const data=new Uint8Array(e.target.result);_excelWorkbook=XLSX.read(data,{type:'array'});showExcelStatus(`✓ <strong>${file.name}</strong> — ${_excelWorkbook.SheetNames.length} sayfa`,false);$('parse-excel-btn').disabled=false;toast('Excel yüklendi');}catch(err){showExcelStatus('Hata: '+err.message,true);}};reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);}

function parseExcelFile(){if(!_excelWorkbook){toast('Excel yükle',true);return;}const periods={};_excelWorkbook.SheetNames.forEach(sn=>{if(!sn.toUpperCase().includes('DÖNEM'))return;const sheet=_excelWorkbook.Sheets[sn];const data=XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet,{header:1,defval:null,blankrows:true,raw:true});const pd=parseSinglePeriod(data,sn);if(pd)periods[sn]=pd;});if(!Object.keys(periods).length){showExcelStatus('Dönem bulunamadı',true);return;}S.excelData=periods;S.viewMode.period='';S.viewMode.month='';applyViewMode();persist();renderAdmin();const c=Object.keys(periods).length;showExcelStatus(`✓ ${c} dönem yüklendi`,false);toast(`${c} dönem ✓`);// Workbook parse sonrası bellekten serbest bırak

_excelWorkbook=null;}

function showExcelStatus(html,isErr){const el=$('excel-status');el.innerHTML=html;el.className='excel-status show'+(isErr?' err':'');}

 

function parseSinglePeriod(data,sheetName){

  try{

    const colors=['#f9c80e','#4ecdc4','#45b7d1','#ff6b35','#a78bfa'];

    const nameRow=findNameRow(data);

    const nameCols=findNameCols(data,nameRow);

    const metricStartRow=nameRow+2;

    const danMetricRows=[metricStartRow,metricStartRow+1,metricStartRow+2,metricStartRow+3,metricStartRow+4];

    let vocScoreCellR=11;

    for(let r=8;r<16;r++){if(data[r]&&data[r][0]&&String(data[r][0]).toLowerCase().includes('endeks'))vocScoreCellR=r;}

    const metricsBase=XL.metricRows.map((r,i)=>{const v=data[r]&&data[r][XL.metricCol]!=null?data[r][XL.metricCol]:null;return{name:(S.metricLabels&&S.metricLabels[i])||XL.metricNames[i],value:v!==null?pctRound(v):0,color:colors[i]};});

    // 1. SEVİYE HEDEF kolonunu oku (soru satırlarındaki hedef değerleri)

    // Hedefler genelde 3. kolonda (index 3) veya 5. kolonda (index 5) - "1. Seviye Hedef" kolonu

    const thresholds=XL.metricRows.map(r=>{

      // Kolon 3'te hedef var (sheets yapısına göre)

      const v=data[r]&&data[r][3];

      if(v===null||v===undefined)return 80;

      const n=parseFloat(v);

      if(isNaN(n))return 80;

      // Eğer 0-1 arası ise yüzdeye çevir

      return n>1.5?n:Math.round(n*100);

    });

    const vocScore=pct(data[vocScoreCellR]&&data[vocScoreCellR][3]);

    const totalAnket=Math.round(safeNum(data[XL.totalAnketCell[0]]&&data[XL.totalAnketCell[0]][XL.totalAnketCell[1]]));

    const geriDonus=pctRound(data[XL.geriDonusCell[0]]&&data[XL.geriDonusCell[0]][XL.geriDonusCell[1]]||0);

    const defC=['#4ecdc4','#45b7d1','#22c55e','#f9c80e','#ff6b35','#a78bfa'];

    const danismanlar=[];

    nameCols.forEach((nc,i)=>{

      const nameCell=data[nameRow]&&data[nameRow][nc];

      if(!nameCell)return;

      const name=String(nameCell).trim();

      if(name.toUpperCase().includes('ANONİM')||name.toUpperCase().includes('ANONIM'))return;

      const vc=nc+6;

      const vocVal=data[nameRow]&&data[nameRow][vc];

      const vocSc=vocVal!=null?pctRound(vocVal):0;

      const scores=danMetricRows.map(r=>{const v=data[r]&&data[r][nc];return v!=null?pctRound(v):0;});

      const anket=Math.round(safeNum(data[danMetricRows[0]]&&data[danMetricRows[0]][vc]));

      const ex=S.danismanlar&&S.danismanlar.find(d=>normTR(d.name)===normTR(name));

      danismanlar.push({name,voc:vocSc,scores,anket,color:ex?.color||defC[i%6],photo:ex?.photo||''});

    });

    const months={};

    const monthStartScan=metricStartRow+5;

    for(let r=monthStartScan;r<data.length;r++){

      const cell=data[r]&&data[r][0];if(!cell)continue;

      const n=normTR(cell);const idx=XL.monthKWNorm.indexOf(n);if(idx<0)continue;

      const bayiVoc=pctRound(data[r]&&data[r][9]||0);

      const mm=[];

      for(let mi=0;mi<5;mi++){const v=data[r+1+mi]&&data[r+1+mi][15];mm.push({name:(S.metricLabels&&S.metricLabels[mi])||XL.metricNames[mi],value:v!=null?pctRound(v):0,color:colors[mi]});}

      const mAnket=Math.round(safeNum(data[r+1]&&data[r+1][9]));

      const mDan=[];

      nameCols.forEach((nc,i)=>{

        const nameC=data[nameRow]&&data[nameRow][nc];

        if(!nameC)return;

        const name=String(nameC).trim();

        if(name.toUpperCase().includes('ANONİM')||name.toUpperCase().includes('ANONIM'))return;

        const vc=nc+6;

        const dV=pctRound(data[r]&&data[r][vc]||0);

        const dS=[];

        for(let mi=0;mi<5;mi++){const v=data[r+1+mi]&&data[r+1+mi][nc];dS.push(v!=null?pctRound(v):0);}

        const dA=Math.round(safeNum(data[r+1]&&data[r+1][vc]));

        const ex=S.danismanlar&&S.danismanlar.find(d=>normTR(d.name)===normTR(name));

        mDan.push({name,voc:dV,scores:dS,anket:dA,color:ex?.color||defC[i%6],photo:ex?.photo||''});

      });

      months[n]={label:String(cell).trim(),voc:bayiVoc,metrics:mm,anket:mAnket,danismanlar:mDan};

    }

    return{sheetName,vocScore,totalAnket,geriDonus,metrics:metricsBase,danismanlar,months,thresholds};

  }catch(err){console.error('parse err:',err);return null;}

}

function setViewMode(m){S.viewMode.mode=m;['vm-auto','vm-period','vm-month'].forEach(id=>$(id)?.classList.remove('active'));$('vm-'+m)?.classList.add('active');$('view-mode-period-options').style.display=m==='period'?'block':'none';$('view-mode-month-options').style.display=m==='month'?'block':'none';applyViewMode();refreshViewModeUI();}

function refreshViewModeUI(){populatePeriodSelects();$('info-today').textContent=new Date().toLocaleDateString('tr-TR',{day:'numeric',month:'long',year:'numeric'});$('info-showing').textContent='Gösterilen: '+(S.currentShowing||'—');}

function populatePeriodSelects(){['view-period-select','view-period-select-2'].forEach(id=>{const sel=$(id);if(!sel)return;sel.innerHTML='';if(!S.excelData){sel.innerHTML='<option>Önce Excel</option>';return;}Object.keys(S.excelData).forEach(pn=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=pn;o.textContent=pn;sel.appendChild(o);});if(S.viewMode.period)sel.value=S.viewMode.period;sel.onchange=()=>{S.viewMode.period=sel.value;if(id==='view-period-select-2'){S.viewMode.month='';updateMonthOptions();}applyViewMode();renderAdmin();refreshViewModeUI();};});updateMonthOptions();const msel=$('view-month-select');if(msel)msel.onchange=()=>{S.viewMode.month=msel.value;applyViewMode();renderAdmin();refreshViewModeUI();};}

function updateMonthOptions(){

  const sel=$('view-month-select');

  const btnContainer=$('month-buttons');

  if(!sel)return;

  sel.innerHTML='';

  if(btnContainer)btnContainer.innerHTML='';

  const pname=$('view-period-select-2')?.value;

  if(!S.excelData||!S.excelData[pname]){sel.innerHTML='<option>—</option>';return;}

  const mk=Object.keys(S.excelData[pname].months);

  const monthDisplayNames={'OCAK':'Ocak','SUBAT':'Şubat','MART':'Mart','NISAN':'Nisan','MAYIS':'Mayıs','HAZIRAN':'Haziran','TEMMUZ':'Temmuz','AGUSTOS':'Ağustos','EYLUL':'Eylül','EKIM':'Ekim','KASIM':'Kasım','ARALIK':'Aralık'};

  mk.forEach(k=>{

    const label=S.excelData[pname].months[k].label||monthDisplayNames[k]||k;

    // Dropdown (hidden, for compatibility)

    const o=document.createElement('option');

    o.value=k;o.textContent=label;sel.appendChild(o);

    // Button

    if(btnContainer){

      const btn=document.createElement('button');

      btn.className='vmb'+(S.viewMode.month===k?' active':'');

      btn.textContent=label;

      btn.onclick=()=>{

        S.viewMode.month=k;sel.value=k;

        applyViewMode();renderAdmin();refreshViewModeUI();

        // Update button active states

        btnContainer.querySelectorAll('.vmb').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));

        btn.classList.add('active');

      };

      btnContainer.appendChild(btn);

    }

  });

  if(S.viewMode.month&&mk.includes(S.viewMode.month))sel.value=S.viewMode.month;

  else if(mk.length){sel.value=mk[0];S.viewMode.month=mk[0];}

}

function extractDonemNo(sn){const m=/(\d+)\.\s*DÖNEM/i.exec(sn);return m?parseInt(m[1]):0;}

function applyViewMode(){

  if(!S.excelData)return;

  let cp=null,cm=null;

  const mode=S.viewMode.mode||'auto';

  if(mode==='auto'){

    const now=new Date();const mi=now.getMonth();const wantDno=Math.floor(mi/3)+1;

    const periods=Object.keys(S.excelData).map(k=>({name:k,no:extractDonemNo(k)}));

    const match=periods.find(p=>p.no===wantDno);

    console.log('[ViewMode] auto: ay='+mi+' wantDno='+wantDno+' periods='+JSON.stringify(periods)+' match='+JSON.stringify(match));

    if(match){cp=match.name;const mn=XL.monthKWNorm[mi];console.log('[ViewMode] monthKey='+mn+' months='+JSON.stringify(Object.keys(S.excelData[cp]?.months||{})));if(S.excelData[cp].months&&S.excelData[cp].months[mn])cm=mn;}

    if(!cp){periods.sort((a,b)=>b.no-a.no);cp=periods[0]?.name||Object.keys(S.excelData)[0];}

  }else if(mode==='period'){cp=S.viewMode.period||Object.keys(S.excelData)[0];}

  else{cp=S.viewMode.period||Object.keys(S.excelData)[0];cm=S.viewMode.month||null;}

  S.viewMode.period=cp;S.viewMode.month=cm;

  applyViewModeFor(cp,cm);

}

// cp/cm verilen dönem+ay için ekran verisini hesaplar (S.viewMode'a DOKUNMAZ).

// Böylece kiosk-yerel geçersiz kılma (LV) yayındaki (published) ayarı bozmadan kullanılabilir.

function applyViewModeFor(cp,cm){

  console.log('[ViewMode] Seçilen: dönem='+cp+' ay='+cm);

  const pd=S.excelData[cp];if(!pd)return;

  const periodDan=(pd.danismanlar&&pd.danismanlar.length)?pd.danismanlar:S.danismanlar;

  S._noData=false; // reset no-data flag

  const hdef=S.hedef||87;

  S._thresholds=pd.thresholds||[hdef,hdef,hdef,hdef,hdef]; // default fallback

  S._monthVoc=null; // aylık VOC (büyük VOC'un altında gösterilecek)

  if(cm&&pd.months&&pd.months[cm]){

    const m=pd.months[cm];

    console.log('[ViewMode] Ay verisi bulundu: voc='+m.voc+' anket='+m.anket+' metrics='+JSON.stringify(m.metrics?.map(x=>x.value))+' danismanlar='+JSON.stringify(m.danismanlar?.map(d=>d.name+':'+d.voc)));

    const hasData=m.metrics&&m.metrics.some(x=>x.value&&x.value>0);

    if(!hasData){

      S._noData=true;

      S.metrics=(pd.metrics||[]).map((x,i)=>({...x,name:(S.metricLabels&&S.metricLabels[i])||x.name,_empty:true}));

      S.voc.score=pd.vocScore; // dönem geneli her zaman

      S._monthVoc=null;

      S.anket.toplam=pd.totalAnket;S.anket.ay=0;S.anket.geri=pd.geriDonusOran||0;

      S.danismanlar=periodDan.map(d=>({...d,voc:0,scores:[0,0,0,0,0],_empty:true}));

      S.currentShowing=`${cp} — ${m.label} (Veri yok)`;

    }else{

      S.metrics=m.metrics.map((x,i)=>({...x,name:(S.metricLabels&&S.metricLabels[i])||x.name}));

      S.voc.score=pd.vocScore; // büyük VOC = dönem geneli

      S._monthVoc=m.voc; // küçük VOC = ay

      S.anket.toplam=pd.totalAnket;S.anket.ay=m.anket||0;S.anket.geri=pd.geriDonusOran||0;

      S.danismanlar=(m.danismanlar&&m.danismanlar.length)?m.danismanlar:periodDan;

      S.currentShowing=`${cp} — ${m.label}`;

      console.log('[ViewMode] AY VERİSİ UYGULANDI: metrics='+JSON.stringify(S.metrics.map(x=>x.name+':'+x.value))+' danismanlar='+JSON.stringify(S.danismanlar.map(d=>d.name+':'+d.voc)));

    }

  }else{

    console.log('[ViewMode] Ay yok, DÖNEM GENELİ: vocScore='+pd.vocScore+' metrics='+JSON.stringify(pd.metrics?.map(x=>x.value))+' danismanlar='+JSON.stringify(pd.danismanlar?.map(d=>d.name+':'+d.voc)));

    S.metrics=pd.metrics.map((x,i)=>({...x,name:(S.metricLabels&&S.metricLabels[i])||x.name}));

    S.voc.score=pd.vocScore;S.anket.toplam=pd.totalAnket;S.anket.ay=pd.totalAnket;S.anket.geri=pd.geriDonusOran||0;

    S.danismanlar=periodDan;

    S.currentShowing=`${cp} (Özet)`;

  }

  // Danışmanları VOC'a göre sırala (en yüksek başta)

  if(S.danismanlar&&S.danismanlar.length)S.danismanlar.sort((a,b)=>(b.voc||0)-(a.voc||0));

  S.trend=[];

  Object.keys(S.excelData).forEach(pn=>{const pdd=S.excelData[pn];if(pdd.months)Object.keys(pdd.months).forEach(mk=>{S.trend.push({label:pdd.months[mk].label.slice(0,3).toUpperCase(),value:pdd.months[mk].voc||0,cur:cm&&mk===cm});});});

  const topD=[...(S.danismanlar||[])].sort((a,b)=>b.voc-a.voc)[0];

  // Ticker: sadece otomatik modda güncelle, kullanıcı elle değiştirdiyse dokunma

  if(!S.ticker._manual){

    S.ticker.items=[`VOC %${Math.round(S.voc.score*10)/10}`,`Toplam ${S.anket.toplam} Anket`,topD?`En Yüksek: ${topD.name} %${Math.round((topD.voc||0)*10)/10}`:'',`Gösterilen: ${S.currentShowing}`].filter(Boolean);

  }

}

 

// =================================================================

// 48 SAAT ARAMA (Excel)

// =================================================================

const MOOD_MAP={'FFFF0000':{key:'kirmizi',label:'OLUMSUZ',color:'#ef4444'},'FF00B050':{key:'yesil',label:'OLUMLU',color:'#22c55e'},'FFFFC000':{key:'turuncu',label:'UYARI',color:'#f59e0b'}};

function detectMood(rgb){if(!rgb||rgb==='00000000')return null;const up=String(rgb).toUpperCase();if(MOOD_MAP[up])return MOOD_MAP[up];return null;}

function handleCall48hSelect(inp){const f=inp.files[0];if(f)readCall48hFile(f);}

function handleCall48hDrop(e){e.preventDefault();e.currentTarget.classList.remove('drag');const f=e.dataTransfer.files[0];if(f&&(f.name.endsWith('.xlsx')||f.name.endsWith('.xls')))readCall48hFile(f);}

// XLSX bir ZIP arşivi - SheetJS Community fill renkleri okumuyor, kendimiz XML parse edeceğiz

async function parseXlsxColors(arrayBuffer){

  // JSZip ile XLSX açıp styles.xml + sheet XML'lerini oku

  const zip=await JSZip.loadAsync(arrayBuffer);

  const files={};

  const names=Object.keys(zip.files);

  for(const n of names){

    if(n.endsWith('.xml')||n.endsWith('.rels'))files[n]=await zip.files[n].async('string');

  }

  // styles.xml - namespace'siz regex-temelli parse (daha güvenilir)

  const stylesXml=files['xl/styles.xml']||'';

  const fillColors=[];

  const cellXfs=[];

  // <fill> elemanlarını sırayla bul

  const fillMatches=stylesXml.match(/<fill>[\s\S]*?<\/fill>/g)||[];

  for(const fill of fillMatches){

    const fgMatch=fill.match(/<fgColor\s+[^>]*rgb="([^"]+)"/);

    fillColors.push(fgMatch?fgMatch[1]:null);

  }

  // <cellXfs> içindeki her <xf>'in fillId'sini al

  const cellXfsBlock=stylesXml.match(/<cellXfs[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/cellXfs>/);

  if(cellXfsBlock){

    const xfs=cellXfsBlock[1].match(/<xf\s+[^>]*?\/?>/g)||[];

    for(const xf of xfs){

      const m=xf.match(/fillId="(\d+)"/);

      cellXfs.push(m?parseInt(m[1]):0);

    }

  }

  // Workbook + rels regex ile - sheet adı -> dosya yolu

  const workbookXml=files['xl/workbook.xml']||'';

  const relsXml=files['xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels']||'';

  const sheetMap={};

  const rels={};

  const relMatches=relsXml.match(/<Relationship\s+[^>]*?\/?>/g)||[];

  for(const r of relMatches){

    const id=r.match(/Id="([^"]+)"/);

    const target=r.match(/Target="([^"]+)"/);

    if(id&&target)rels[id[1]]=target[1];

  }

  const sheetMatches=workbookXml.match(/<sheet\s+[^>]*?\/?>/g)||[];

  for(const s of sheetMatches){

    const name=s.match(/name="([^"]+)"/);

    const rid=s.match(/r:id="([^"]+)"/);

    if(name&&rid){

      let target=rels[rid[1]]||'';

      if(target&&!target.startsWith('xl/'))target='xl/'+target;

      sheetMap[name[1]]=target;

    }

  }

  // Her sayfanın XML'inden A kolonu hücrelerinin style index'ini oku

  const sheetColors={};

  for(const sn in sheetMap){

    const path=sheetMap[sn];

    const xml=files[path]||'';

    if(!xml)continue;

    sheetColors[sn]={};

    // Her <row r="N"> bloğunu yakala

    const rowRegex=/<row\s+([^>]*)>([\s\S]*?)<\/row>/g;

    let rowMatch;

    while((rowMatch=rowRegex.exec(xml))!==null){

      const rAttr=rowMatch[1].match(/r="(\d+)"/);

      if(!rAttr)continue;

      const rNum=parseInt(rAttr[1])-1;

      const rowContent=rowMatch[2];

      // A kolonu hücresi: r="A<num>"

      const cellMatch=rowContent.match(/<c\s+r="A\d+"[^>]*s="(\d+)"/);

      if(cellMatch){

        const styleIdx=parseInt(cellMatch[1]);

        if(styleIdx>=0&&cellXfs[styleIdx]!==undefined){

          const fillIdx=cellXfs[styleIdx];

          const rgb=fillColors[fillIdx];

          if(rgb)sheetColors[sn][rNum]=rgb;

        }

      }

    }

  }

  return sheetColors;

}

 

async function readCall48hFile(file){

  showCall48hStatus('Renkler okunuyor...',false);

  const reader=new FileReader();

  reader.onload=async e=>{

    try{

      const data=new Uint8Array(e.target.result);

      // 1. SheetJS ile verileri oku

      const wb=XLSX.read(data,{type:'array'});

      // 2. ZIP açıp renk bilgilerini al

      let sheetColors={};

      try{sheetColors=await parseXlsxColors(e.target.result);}catch(err){console.warn('Renk okuma hatası:',err);}

      const parsed={};

      wb.SheetNames.forEach(sn=>{

        const dayNum=parseInt(sn);

        if(isNaN(dayNum)||dayNum<1||dayNum>31)return;

        const sheet=wb.Sheets[sn];

        const rows=XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet,{header:1,defval:'',blankrows:false,raw:true});

        if(rows.length<2)return;

        const colorsForSheet=sheetColors[sn]||{};

        const items=[];

        for(let r=1;r<rows.length;r++){

          const row=rows[r];

          if(!row||!row.some(x=>x&&String(x).trim()))continue;

          const danisman=String(row[4]||'').trim();

          const aciklama=String(row[8]||'').trim();

          if(!danisman&&!aciklama)continue;

          const rgb=colorsForSheet[r];

          const mood=rgb?detectMood(rgb):null;

          // Dolgusuz olanları ATLA - kullanıcının isteği

          if(!mood)continue;

          items.push({skfNo:String(row[0]||'').trim(),is:String(row[1]||'').trim(),plaka:String(row[2]||'').trim(),musteri:String(row[3]||'').trim(),danisman,operator:[row[5],row[6],row[7]].filter(x=>x&&String(x).trim()).map(x=>String(x).trim()).join(', '),aciklama,mood:mood.key,moodLabel:mood.label,moodColor:mood.color,show:false});

        }

        if(items.length)parsed[dayNum]=items;

      });

      if(!Object.keys(parsed).length){showCall48hStatus('Gün bulunamadı',true);return;}

      S.calls48h.data=parsed;

      const total=Object.values(parsed).reduce((a,b)=>a+b.length,0);

      const kirmizi=Object.values(parsed).flat().filter(x=>x.mood==='kirmizi').length;

      const yesil=Object.values(parsed).flat().filter(x=>x.mood==='yesil').length;

      showCall48hStatus(`✓ <strong>${file.name}</strong> - ${Object.keys(parsed).length} gün, ${total} kayıt (${kirmizi} kırmızı, ${yesil} yeşil)`,false);

      // Workbook + zip parse sonrası bellekten serbest bırak

      wb.Sheets=null; wb.SheetNames=null;

      persist();render48hAdmin();toast('48 Saat ✓');

    }catch(err){showCall48hStatus('Hata: '+err.message,true);console.error(err);}

  };

  reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);

}

function showCall48hStatus(html,err){const el=$('call48h-status');if(el){el.innerHTML=html;el.className='excel-status show'+(err?' err':'');}}

 

function render48hAdmin(){

  const monSel=$('call48h-month'),yrSel=$('call48h-year'),modeSel=$('call48h-mode'),spdR=$('calls-speed-range'),spdL=$('calls-speed-lbl');

  if(monSel){monSel.value=S.calls48h.selectedMonth;monSel.onchange=()=>{S.calls48h.selectedMonth=+monSel.value;};}

  if(yrSel){yrSel.value=S.calls48h.selectedYear;yrSel.onchange=()=>{S.calls48h.selectedYear=+yrSel.value;};}

  if(modeSel){

    modeSel.value=S.calls48h.selectedDay==='auto'?'auto':(S.calls48h.manualMode?'manual':'day');

    modeSel.onchange=()=>{const m=modeSel.value;$('call48h-day-wrap').style.display=(m==='day'||m==='manual')?'block':'none';S.calls48h.manualMode=(m==='manual');if(m==='auto')S.calls48h.selectedDay='auto';render48hTable();};

  }

  if(spdR){spdR.value=S.calls48h.scrollSpeed||35;spdL.textContent=spdR.value;spdR.oninput=()=>{S.calls48h.scrollSpeed=+spdR.value;spdL.textContent=spdR.value;};}

  const tabsEl=$('call48h-day-tabs');

  if(tabsEl){

    tabsEl.innerHTML='';

    const days=Object.keys(S.calls48h.data||{}).map(x=>+x).sort((a,b)=>a-b);

    if(days.length===0)tabsEl.innerHTML='<div style="color:var(--muted);font-size:11px">Henüz veri yok</div>';

    else days.forEach(d=>{const b=document.createElement('button');b.className='vmb'+(+S.calls48h.selectedDay===d?' active':'');b.style.padding='5px 11px';b.style.fontSize='11px';b.textContent=d;b.onclick=()=>{S.calls48h.selectedDay=d;render48hAdmin();};tabsEl.appendChild(b);});

  }

  const mode=modeSel?modeSel.value:'auto';

  $('call48h-day-wrap').style.display=(mode==='day'||mode==='manual')?'block':'none';

  render48hTable();

}

function render48hTable(){

  const wrap=$('call48h-table-wrap');if(!wrap)return;

  const day=S.calls48h.selectedDay==='auto'?null:+S.calls48h.selectedDay;

  if(!S.calls48h.data||!Object.keys(S.calls48h.data).length){wrap.innerHTML='<div style="padding:24px;text-align:center;color:var(--muted);font-size:13px">Henüz Excel yüklenmedi</div>';return;}

  let items=[];

  let activeDay=day;

  if(day&&S.calls48h.data[day])items=S.calls48h.data[day];

  else if(Object.keys(S.calls48h.data).length){activeDay=+Object.keys(S.calls48h.data)[0];items=S.calls48h.data[activeDay];}

  if(!items.length){wrap.innerHTML='<div style="padding:24px;text-align:center;color:var(--muted);font-size:13px">Seçili günde kayıt yok</div>';return;}

  // Toplu seçim butonları + bilgi satırı

  const checkedCount=items.filter(x=>x.show).length;

  let html='<div style="padding:10px 14px;background:var(--bg3);border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;font-size:12px">';

  html+=`<strong style="color:var(--accent)">Manuel Seçim:</strong> ${checkedCount}/${items.length} işaretli`;

  html+='<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-xs" onclick="bulk48h(true)">Tümünü Seç</button>';

  html+='<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-xs" onclick="bulk48h(false)">Tümünü Bırak</button>';

  html+='<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-xs" onclick="bulk48hMood(\'kirmizi\')">Kırmızıları Seç</button>';

  html+='<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-xs" onclick="bulk48hMood(\'yesil\')">Yeşilleri Seç</button>';

  html+='<span style="margin-left:auto;color:var(--muted);font-size:11px">"Göster" işaretli olanlar kioskta görünür. Mod=Manuel ise sadece bunlar; Otomatik/Belirli gün modunda işaretler ek filtre olarak çalışır.</span>';

  html+='</div>';

  html+='<table class="tbl"><thead><tr><th style="width:50px">Göster</th><th style="width:90px">Renk</th><th>Danışman</th><th>Müşteri</th><th>Plaka</th><th>Açıklama</th></tr></thead><tbody>';

  items.forEach((it,i)=>{

    const chk=`<td style="text-align:center"><input type="checkbox" class="call48h-show" data-idx="${i}" data-day="${activeDay}" ${it.show?'checked':''} style="width:18px;height:18px;cursor:pointer;accent-color:var(--accent)"></td>`;

    const dot=it.mood!=='notr'?`<span style="display:inline-block;width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:50%;background:${it.moodColor};margin-right:6px;vertical-align:middle;box-shadow:0 0 6px ${it.moodColor}"></span>`:'';

    const lblColor=it.mood!=='notr'?`color:${it.moodColor};font-weight:700`:'color:var(--muted)';

    const txt=(it.aciklama||'').substring(0,120)+((it.aciklama||'').length>120?'...':'');

    const rowBg=it.mood==='kirmizi'?'background:rgba(239,68,68,0.05)':(it.mood==='yesil'?'background:rgba(34,197,94,0.04)':'');

    html+=`<tr style="${rowBg}">${chk}<td><span style="${lblColor};font-size:11px">${dot}${it.moodLabel||'NÖTR'}</span></td><td><strong>${it.danisman||'—'}</strong></td><td>${it.musteri||'—'}</td><td style="font-family:monospace;font-size:11px">${it.plaka||'—'}</td><td style="max-width:350px;font-size:12px;line-height:1.4">${txt}</td></tr>`;

  });

  html+='</tbody></table>';

  wrap.innerHTML=html;

  wrap.querySelectorAll('.call48h-show').forEach(cb=>{cb.onchange=()=>{const idx=+cb.dataset.idx;const d=+cb.dataset.day;if(S.calls48h.data[d]&&S.calls48h.data[d][idx]){S.calls48h.data[d][idx].show=cb.checked;render48hTable();}};});

}

function bulk48h(val){const day=S.calls48h.selectedDay==='auto'?+Object.keys(S.calls48h.data)[0]:+S.calls48h.selectedDay;const items=S.calls48h.data[day]||[];items.forEach(it=>it.show=val);render48hTable();toast(val?'Tümü seçildi':'Tümü bırakıldı');}

function bulk48hMood(moodKey){const day=S.calls48h.selectedDay==='auto'?+Object.keys(S.calls48h.data)[0]:+S.calls48h.selectedDay;const items=S.calls48h.data[day]||[];items.forEach(it=>{if(it.mood===moodKey)it.show=true;});render48hTable();toast('İşaretlendi');}

function pick48hForDisplay(){

  if(!S.calls48h||!S.calls48h.data)return{items:[],dateStr:''};

  const data=S.calls48h.data;

  const now=new Date();

  let isLocal=false,day=null;

  // Bu cihaza özel yerel gün seçimi varsa (yayını etkilemez), onu kullan

  if(LV.calls48h&&LV.calls48h.active&&LV.calls48h.day&&data[+LV.calls48h.day]){

    day=+LV.calls48h.day;isLocal=true;

  }

  // Otomatik modda her zaman bugünün ay/yılını kullan

  if(S.calls48h.selectedDay==='auto'){

    S.calls48h.selectedMonth=now.getMonth()+1;

    S.calls48h.selectedYear=now.getFullYear();

  }

  const month=S.calls48h.selectedMonth||now.getMonth()+1;

  const year=S.calls48h.selectedYear||now.getFullYear();

  // Hangi gün gösterilecek?

  if(!isLocal){

    if(S.calls48h.selectedDay==='auto'){const today=now.getDate();const avail=Object.keys(data).map(x=>+x).sort((a,b)=>b-a);day=avail.find(d=>d<=today)||avail[0];}

    else day=+S.calls48h.selectedDay;

  }

  if(!day||!data[day])return{items:[],dateStr:''};

  const dateStr=`${String(day).padStart(2,'0')}.${String(month).padStart(2,'0')}.${year}`+(isLocal?' 📍 Yerel':'');

  const allItems=data[day];

  // Manuel seçim varsa SADECE işaretli olanlar gösterilir

  const manualPicks=allItems.filter(x=>x.show);

  if(manualPicks.length){return{items:manualPicks,dateStr:dateStr+' — Manuel',day};}

  // İşaretli yoksa OTOMATIK seçim: her danışman için 1 kırmızı + 1 yeşil

  const byDan={};

  allItems.forEach(it=>{const d=it.danisman||'?';if(!byDan[d])byDan[d]={kirmizi:null,yesil:null,turuncu:null};if(it.mood==='kirmizi'&&!byDan[d].kirmizi)byDan[d].kirmizi=it;if(it.mood==='yesil'&&!byDan[d].yesil)byDan[d].yesil=it;if(it.mood==='turuncu'&&!byDan[d].turuncu)byDan[d].turuncu=it;});

  const picks=[];

  Object.keys(byDan).forEach(d=>{if(byDan[d].kirmizi)picks.push(byDan[d].kirmizi);if(byDan[d].yesil)picks.push(byDan[d].yesil);if(!byDan[d].kirmizi&&!byDan[d].yesil&&byDan[d].turuncu)picks.push(byDan[d].turuncu);});

  return{items:picks,dateStr:dateStr,day};

}

}

function setBgType(type){S.bg.type=type;['bg-solid','bg-image','bg-video'].forEach(id=>$(id).classList.remove('active'));$('bg-'+type).classList.add('active');$('bg-solid-opts').style.display=type==='solid'?'block':'none';$('bg-image-opts').style.display=type==='image'?'block':'none';$('bg-video-opts').style.display=type==='video'?'block':'none';}

function setKioskBg(c,el){S.bg.color=c;document.querySelectorAll('#kiosk-bg-presets .cp').forEach(p=>p.classList.remove('sel'));if(el)el.classList.add('sel');$('kiosk-bg-pick').value=c;$('kiosk-bg-hex').textContent=c;}

function loadBgImages(inp){

  const files=Array.from(inp.files);

  if(!files.length)return;

  let n=0;

  files.forEach(f=>{

    const r=new FileReader();

    r.onload=async e=>{

      const idx=S.bg.images.length;

      S.bg.images.push(e.target.result);

      try{await idbSet('bg_image_'+idx,e.target.result);}catch(err){console.warn('bg img idb:',err);}

      n++;

      if(n===files.length){renderBgImageList();persist();toast(`${files.length} resim ✓`);}

    };

    r.readAsDataURL(f);

  });

}

function dropBgImage(e){e.preventDefault();e.currentTarget.classList.remove('drag');const files=Array.from(e.dataTransfer.files).filter(f=>f.type.startsWith('image/'));if(files.length)loadBgImages({files});}

function renderBgImageList(){const c=$('bg-image-list');c.innerHTML='';S.bg.images.forEach((img,i)=>{const d=document.createElement('div');d.style.cssText='display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:8px;background:var(--bg3);border-radius:8px;margin-bottom:8px';d.innerHTML=`<img src="${img}" style="width:60px;height:40px;object-fit:cover;border-radius:6px"><span style="flex:1;font-size:12px;color:var(--muted)">Resim ${i+1}</span><button class="btn btn-red btn-xs" onclick="removeBgImage(${i})">✕</button>`;c.appendChild(d);});}

function removeBgImage(i){S.bg.images.splice(i,1);renderBgImageList();}

function loadBgVideo(inp){const f=inp.files[0];if(!f)return;if(f.size>200*1024*1024){toast('Video 200MB üstü',true);return;}const r=new FileReader();r.onload=async e=>{S.bg.video=e.target.result;try{await idbSet('bg_video',e.target.result);$('bg-video-status').style.display='block';$('bg-video-status').innerHTML=`<div style="padding:10px;background:rgba(78,205,196,0.1);border-radius:8px;color:#4ecdc4;font-size:12px">✓ ${(f.size/1024/1024).toFixed(1)} MB</div>`;toast('Video ✓');}catch(err){toast('IDB hata',true);}};r.readAsDataURL(f);}

function dropBgVideo(e){e.preventDefault();e.currentTarget.classList.remove('drag');const f=e.dataTransfer.files[0];if(f&&f.type.startsWith('video/'))loadBgVideo({files:[f]});}

function clearBgVideo(){S.bg.video='';idbDel('bg_video');$('bg-video-status').style.display='none';toast('Kaldırıldı');}

function renderBgOptions(){setBgType(S.bg.type||'solid');$('kiosk-bg-pick').value=S.bg.color;$('kiosk-bg-hex').textContent=S.bg.color;$('bg-opacity-range').value=S.bg.opacity;$('bg-opacity-lbl').textContent=S.bg.opacity;$('bg-slide-dur-range').value=S.bg.slideDur;$('bg-slide-dur-lbl').textContent=S.bg.slideDur;renderBgImageList();if(S.bg.video){$('bg-video-status').style.display='block';$('bg-video-status').innerHTML=`<div style="padding:10px;background:rgba(78,205,196,0.1);border-radius:8px;color:#4ecdc4;font-size:12px">✓ Video yüklü</div>`;}}

 

function renderDan(){const tb=$('dan-tbody');tb.innerHTML='';S.danismanlar.forEach((d,i)=>{const tr=document.createElement('tr');const av=d.photo?`<img src="${d.photo}">`:`<span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:${d.color}">${inits(d.name)}</span>`;const sc=d.scores||[0,0,0,0,0];tr.innerHTML=`<td><div class="av-wrap" onclick="document.getElementById('avf${i}').click()">${av}</div><input type="file" id="avf${i}" accept="image/*" style="display:none" onchange="loadAv(this,${i})"></td><td><input class="f-input" type="text" value="${d.name}" onchange="S.danismanlar[${i}].name=this.value"></td><td><input class="f-input" type="number" value="${d.voc||0}" min="0" max="100" style="width:56px;color:var(--accent);font-weight:700" onchange="S.danismanlar[${i}].voc=+this.value"></td>${[0,1,2,3,4].map(j=>`<td><input class="f-input" type="number" value="${sc[j]||0}" min="0" max="100" onchange="S.danismanlar[${i}].scores[${j}]=+this.value"></td>`).join('')}<td><input class="f-input" type="number" value="${d.anket||0}" style="width:52px" onchange="S.danismanlar[${i}].anket=+this.value"></td><td><input type="color" value="${d.color}" onchange="S.danismanlar[${i}].color=this.value"></td><td><button class="btn btn-red btn-xs" onclick="S.danismanlar.splice(${i},1);renderDan()">✕</button></td>`;tb.appendChild(tr);});}

function loadAv(inp,i){

  const f=inp.files[0];if(!f)return;

  const r=new FileReader();

  r.onload=async e=>{

    const dataUrl=e.target.result;

    // GitHub token varsa hemen yükle ve URL olarak sakla

    if(_ghKey()){

      try{

        toast('Fotoğraf yükleniyor...');

        let img=dataUrl;

        if(img.length>500000)img=await compressImage(img,300,0.82);

        const b64=dataUrlToBase64(img);

        const path='assets/danisman_'+i+'.jpg';

        await ghPutFile(path,b64,'Update danisman '+i);

        const rawBase='https://raw.githubusercontent.com/'+GH_REPO+'/'+GH_BRANCH+'/';

        S.danismanlar[i].photo=rawBase+path+'?t='+Date.now();

        S.danismanlar[i].photoPath=path;

        toast('✓ Fotoğraf yüklendi');

      }catch(err){

        // GitHub başarısız — geçici olarak base64 kullan

        S.danismanlar[i].photo=dataUrl;

        toast('GitHub yükleme başarısız, geçici kaydedildi',true);

      }

    }else{

      // Token yok — base64 olarak sakla (yayına alırken yüklenir)

      S.danismanlar[i].photo=dataUrl;

    }

    renderDan();

  };

  r.readAsDataURL(f);

}

 

function renderPdfs(){const c=$('pdf-list');c.innerHTML='';S.pdfs.forEach((p,i)=>{const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='pdf-row pdf-admin-row';d.dataset.pdfdata=p.data||'';d.dataset.idx=i;const hasF=!!p.data;d.innerHTML=`<div class="pdf-row-head"><div class="pdf-num">${i+1}</div><input class="f-input pdf-t" type="text" value="${p.title}" style="flex:1"><button class="btn btn-red btn-xs" onclick="removePdfRow(${i})">✕</button></div><input class="f-input pdf-u" type="url" value="${p.url||''}" placeholder="https://..." style="margin-bottom:8px"><div class="pdf-drop-zone ${hasF?'has-file':''}" onclick="document.getElementById('pdff${i}').click()" ondragover="event.preventDefault();this.classList.add('drag')" ondragleave="this.classList.remove('drag')" ondrop="dropPdf(event,${i})">${hasF?'✓ Yüklendi (kalıcı)':'PDF sürükle / tıkla'}</div><input type="file" id="pdff${i}" accept="application/pdf" style="display:none" onchange="loadPdfFile(this,${i})">`;c.appendChild(d);});}

async function removePdfRow(idx){

  if(!confirm('PDF silinsin mi?'))return;

  S.pdfs.splice(idx,1);

  // IDB'den de temizle - tüm pdf_* keys silip yeniden yaz

  try{

    const keys=await idbKeys();

    for(const k of keys){if(k.startsWith('pdf_'))await idbDel(k);}

    for(let i=0;i<S.pdfs.length;i++){if(S.pdfs[i].data)await idbSet('pdf_'+i,S.pdfs[i].data);}

  }catch(e){}

  persist();

  renderPdfs();

  toast('PDF silindi ✓');

}

function addPdf(){S.pdfs.push({title:'Yeni',url:'',data:''});renderPdfs();}

function loadPdfFile(inp,i){const f=inp.files[0];if(!f)return;const r=new FileReader();r.onload=async e=>{const row=document.querySelectorAll('.pdf-admin-row')[i];if(row){row.dataset.pdfdata=e.target.result;const dz=row.querySelector('.pdf-drop-zone');dz.textContent='✓ Yüklendi (kalıcı)';dz.classList.add('has-file');if(!row.querySelector('.pdf-t').value||row.querySelector('.pdf-t').value==='Yeni')row.querySelector('.pdf-t').value=f.name.replace('.pdf','');}try{await idbSet('pdf_'+i,e.target.result);if(S.pdfs[i])S.pdfs[i].data=e.target.result;toast('PDF kalıcı kaydedildi ✓');}catch(err){toast('IDB hata',true);}};r.readAsDataURL(f);}

function dropPdf(e,i){e.preventDefault();e.currentTarget.classList.remove('drag');const f=e.dataTransfer.files[0];if(f&&f.type==='application/pdf')loadPdfFile({files:[f]},i);}

function saveGhToken(){

  const t=document.getElementById('set-gh-token').value.trim();

  if(!t){toast('Token boş olamaz',true);return;}

  if(!t.startsWith('github_pat_')){toast('Geçerli bir token değil',true);return;}

  if(_setGhKey(t)){

    document.getElementById('set-gh-token').value='';

    document.getElementById('gh-token-status').innerHTML='<span style="color:#22c55e;font-weight:700">✓ Kaydedildi</span>';

    setTimeout(()=>{document.getElementById('gh-token-status').innerHTML='';},3000);

    toast('GitHub Token kaydedildi ✓');

  }else{

    toast('Geçersiz format',true);

  }

}

function exportData(){const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(S,null,2)],{type:'application/json'});const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob);a.download='kiosk.json';a.click();}

function importData(inp){const f=inp.files[0];if(!f)return;const r=new FileReader();r.onload=e=>{try{S=JSON.parse(e.target.result);mergeDefaults(S,DEF);persist();renderAdmin();toast('✓');}catch{toast('Hata',true);}};r.readAsText(f);}

 

function collectAdmin(){

  if($('brand-name'))S.brand.name=$('brand-name').value||S.brand.name;

  if($('brand-sub'))S.brand.sub=$('brand-sub').value||S.brand.sub;

  const vmode=S.viewMode.mode||'auto';

  if(vmode==='period'){const v1=$('view-period-select')?.value;if(v1)S.viewMode.period=v1;}

  else if(vmode==='month'){const v2=$('view-period-select-2')?.value;const m=$('view-month-select')?.value;if(v2)S.viewMode.period=v2;if(m)S.viewMode.month=m;}

  const pdfRows=document.querySelectorAll('.pdf-admin-row');

  if(pdfRows.length){

    const ps=[];

    pdfRows.forEach(r=>{

      const idx=+r.dataset.idx;

      const existing=S.pdfs[idx];

      ps.push({title:r.querySelector('.pdf-t').value,url:r.querySelector('.pdf-u').value,data:r.dataset.pdfdata||(existing?existing.data:'')});

    });

    S.pdfs=ps;

  }

  if($('pdf-scroll-range'))S.pdfScrollDur=+$('pdf-scroll-range').value||25;

  if($('pdf-page-range'))S.pdfPageLimit=+$('pdf-page-range').value||3;

  if($('bg-opacity-range'))S.bg.opacity=+$('bg-opacity-range').value||40;

  if($('bg-slide-dur-range'))S.bg.slideDur=+$('bg-slide-dur-range').value||8;

  collectEgitim();

  const metInps=document.querySelectorAll('.metric-label-inp');

  if(metInps.length===5){const arr=[];metInps.forEach((el,i)=>arr.push(el.value.trim()||XL.metricNames[i]));S.metricLabels=arr;}

  const ti=[];document.querySelectorAll('.ticker-inp').forEach(i=>{if(i.value.trim())ti.push(i.value.trim());});if(ti.length){S.ticker.items=ti;S.ticker._manual=true;}

  if($('ticker-speed'))S.ticker.speed=+$('ticker-speed').value||25;

}

 

function renderAdmin(){

  document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme',S.theme);

  document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--accent',S.accent);

  document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--accent-rgb',hexToRgb(S.accent));

  $('theme-btn').textContent=S.theme==='dark'?'☀️ Açık':'🌙 Koyu';

  $('brand-name').value=S.brand.name;$('brand-sub').value=S.brand.sub;

  if(S.brand.logo){$('logo-preview').src=S.brand.logo;$('logo-preview').style.display='block';$('logo-drop-txt').style.display='none';}

  if($('pdf-scroll-range')){$('pdf-scroll-range').value=S.pdfScrollDur;$('pdf-scroll-lbl').textContent=S.pdfScrollDur;}

  if($('pdf-page-range')){$('pdf-page-range').value=S.pdfPageLimit;$('pdf-page-lbl').textContent=S.pdfPageLimit;}

  $('ticker-speed').value=S.ticker.speed;

  $('accent-pick').value=S.accent;$('accent-hex').textContent=S.accent;

  // GitHub token durumunu göster

  if(document.getElementById('gh-token-status')){

    const has=!!_ghKey();

    document.getElementById('gh-token-status').innerHTML=has?'<span style="color:#22c55e;font-weight:700">✓ Token kayıtlı</span>':'<span style="color:#ef4444">⚠ Token yok</span>';

  }

  setViewMode(S.viewMode.mode||'auto');

  renderBgOptions();

  if(!S.metricLabels||S.metricLabels.length!==5)S.metricLabels=XL.metricNames.slice();

  renderWeightsTab();

}

function ensureDesignerFsToolbar(){

  if(document.querySelector('.designer-fs-toolbar'))return;

  const bar=document.createElement('div');

  bar.className='designer-fs-toolbar';

  bar.innerHTML=`

    <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="toggleFsPanel('left')" title="Widget kütüphanesi">📚</button>

    <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="toggleFsPanel('right')" title="Özellikler">⚙️</button>

    <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="toggleGrid()" title="Izgara">⊞</button>

    <button class="btn btn-accent btn-sm" onclick="saveAndKiosk()">▶ Kiosk</button>

    <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="exitDesignerFs()">← Admin</button>

  `;

  document.body.appendChild(bar);

}

function toggleFsPanel(side){

  const cls='hide-'+side;

  document.body.classList.toggle(cls);

  // canvas'ı yeniden boyutlandır - panel gizli ama canvas-scaler yine tüm ekran olduğu için

  // scale değişmez. Yine de refresh edelim.

  requestAnimationFrame(()=>renderDesignerCanvas());

}

function exitDesignerFs(){

  document.body.classList.remove('designer-fs');

  // Varsayılan sekmeye (Excel) dön

  const excelTab=document.querySelector('.adm-nav-item');

  if(excelTab)goTab('excel',excelTab);

}

function renderWidgetPalette(){

  const c=$('widget-palette');if(!c)return;

  c.innerHTML='';

  Object.keys(WIDGETS).forEach(type=>{

    const w=WIDGETS[type];

    const btn=document.createElement('div');

    btn.className='widget-btn';

    btn.draggable=true;

    btn.dataset.type=type;

    btn.innerHTML=`<div class="widget-btn-ico">${w.icon}</div><div><div class="widget-btn-name">${w.name}</div><div class="widget-btn-desc">${w.w}×${w.h} hücre</div></div>`;

    btn.ondragstart=e=>{e.dataTransfer.setData('widget-type',type);e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed='copy';};

    btn.onclick=()=>addWidgetToCanvas(type);

    c.appendChild(btn);

  });

}

 

const CANVAS_REAL={w:1920,h:1080}; // Tasarım mantıksal boyut (kioskla aynı)

function getCanvasSize(){

  // Fullscreen'de viewport tamamı kullanılır (paneller overlay)

  const isFs=document.body.classList.contains('designer-fs');

  let maxW,maxH;

  if(isFs){

    maxW=window.innerWidth-16;

    maxH=window.innerHeight-16;

  }else{

    const scaler=$('canvas-scaler');

    if(!scaler)return{w:CANVAS_REAL.w,h:CANVAS_REAL.h,scale:1};

    const rect=scaler.getBoundingClientRect();

    maxW=rect.width-16;maxH=rect.height-16;

  }

  const scale=Math.min(maxW/CANVAS_REAL.w,maxH/CANVAS_REAL.h);

  return{w:CANVAS_REAL.w,h:CANVAS_REAL.h,scale:scale};

}

 

function renderDesignerCanvas(){

  const canvas=$('designer-canvas');

  if(!canvas)return;

  const size=getCanvasSize();

  const isFs=document.body.classList.contains('designer-fs');

  canvas.style.width=size.w+'px';

  canvas.style.height=size.h+'px';

  canvas.style.transform=`scale(${size.scale})`;

  canvas.style.transformOrigin='top left';

  if(isFs){

    // Viewport'ta ortala

    const scaledW=size.w*size.scale;

    const scaledH=size.h*size.scale;

    const offX=Math.max(0,(window.innerWidth-scaledW)/2);

    const offY=Math.max(0,(window.innerHeight-scaledH)/2);

    canvas.style.position='absolute';

    canvas.style.left=offX+'px';

    canvas.style.top=offY+'px';

    canvas.style.margin='0';

  }else{

    canvas.style.position='';

    canvas.style.left='';

    canvas.style.top='';

    canvas.style.margin='0';

  }

  const scaler=$('canvas-scaler');

  if(scaler)scaler.style.position='relative';

  const bgColor=S.bg.color||'#06080f';

  canvas.style.background=bgColor;

  canvas.innerHTML='';

 

  // Grid overlay

  if(_showGrid){

    const gridEl=document.createElement('div');

    gridEl.className='designer-canvas-grid';

    const cols=S.canvas.gridCols,rows=S.canvas.gridRows;

    const cellW=w/cols,cellH=h/rows;

    for(let y=0;y<rows;y++){

      for(let x=0;x<cols;x++){

        const cell=document.createElement('div');

        cell.className='designer-canvas-grid-cell';

        cell.style.left=(x*cellW)+'px';

        cell.style.top=(y*cellH)+'px';

        cell.style.width=cellW+'px';

        cell.style.height=cellH+'px';

        gridEl.appendChild(cell);

      }

    }

    canvas.appendChild(gridEl);

  }

 

  // Drop zone

  canvas.ondragover=e=>{e.preventDefault();e.dataTransfer.dropEffect='copy';};

  canvas.ondrop=e=>{

    e.preventDefault();

    const type=e.dataTransfer.getData('widget-type');

    if(!type||!WIDGETS[type])return;

    const rect=canvas.getBoundingClientRect();

    const cellW=rect.width/S.canvas.gridCols,cellH=rect.height/S.canvas.gridRows;

    const x=Math.floor((e.clientX-rect.left)/cellW);

    const y=Math.floor((e.clientY-rect.top)/cellH);

    addWidgetToCanvas(type,x,y);

  };

 

  // Deseçtir: boş alana tık

  canvas.onclick=e=>{if(e.target===canvas||e.target.classList.contains('designer-canvas-grid')||e.target.classList.contains('designer-canvas-grid-cell')){_selectedWidget=null;renderDesignerCanvas();renderPropsPanel();}};

 

  // Widget'ları çiz

  (S.canvas.layout||[]).forEach(w=>drawCanvasWidget(canvas,w));

}

 

function drawCanvasWidget(canvas,widget){

  const cw=CANVAS_REAL.w,ch=CANVAS_REAL.h;

  const cellW=cw/S.canvas.gridCols,cellH=ch/S.canvas.gridRows;

  const el=document.createElement('div');

  el.className='canvas-widget designer-mode'+(_selectedWidget===widget.id?' selected':'');

  el.dataset.id=widget.id;

  el.style.left=(widget.x*cellW)+'px';

  el.style.top=(widget.y*cellH)+'px';

  el.style.width=(widget.w*cellW)+'px';

  el.style.height=(widget.h*cellH)+'px';

  el.style.background=widget.bg||'rgba(255,255,255,0.04)';

  el.style.border=widget.border?`1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.15)`:'none';

  el.style.opacity=(widget.opacity??100)/100;

 

  // Move handle (tam kaplıyor)

  const mv=document.createElement('div');

  mv.className='cw-move';

  mv.onmousedown=e=>startDrag(e,widget,'move');

  el.appendChild(mv);

 

  // Widget içeriği (preview, küçük)

  const content=document.createElement('div');

  content.className='canvas-widget-content';

  content.innerHTML=renderWidgetHtml(widget,true);

  el.appendChild(content);

 

  // Resize handles

  ['se','ne','sw','nw'].forEach(dir=>{

    const h=document.createElement('div');

    h.className='resize-handle rh-'+dir;

    h.onmousedown=e=>{e.stopPropagation();startDrag(e,widget,'resize-'+dir);};

    el.appendChild(h);

  });

 

  // Delete button

  const del=document.createElement('button');

  del.className='cw-del';

  del.textContent='✕';

  del.onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();deleteWidget(widget.id);};

  el.appendChild(del);

 

  el.onclick=e=>{

    if(e.target.classList.contains('resize-handle'))return;

    e.stopPropagation();

    _selectedWidget=widget.id;

    renderDesignerCanvas();

    renderPropsPanel();

  };

 

  canvas.appendChild(el);

}

 

function startDrag(e,widget,mode){

  e.preventDefault();

  const canvas=$('designer-canvas');

  const rect=canvas.getBoundingClientRect();

  const cellW=rect.width/S.canvas.gridCols,cellH=rect.height/S.canvas.gridRows;

  _dragState={

    widget:widget,mode:mode,

    startMouseX:e.clientX,startMouseY:e.clientY,

    startX:widget.x,startY:widget.y,

    startW:widget.w,startH:widget.h,

    cellW,cellH,

    maxCols:S.canvas.gridCols,maxRows:S.canvas.gridRows

  };

  _selectedWidget=widget.id;

  document.addEventListener('mousemove',onDrag);

  document.addEventListener('mouseup',endDrag);

}

function onDrag(e){

  if(!_dragState)return;

  const s=_dragState;

  const dx=Math.round((e.clientX-s.startMouseX)/s.cellW);

  const dy=Math.round((e.clientY-s.startMouseY)/s.cellH);

  if(s.mode==='move'){

    s.widget.x=Math.max(0,Math.min(s.maxCols-s.widget.w,s.startX+dx));

    s.widget.y=Math.max(0,Math.min(s.maxRows-s.widget.h,s.startY+dy));

  }else if(s.mode==='resize-se'){

    s.widget.w=Math.max(1,Math.min(s.maxCols-s.widget.x,s.startW+dx));

    s.widget.h=Math.max(1,Math.min(s.maxRows-s.widget.y,s.startH+dy));

  }else if(s.mode==='resize-ne'){

    s.widget.w=Math.max(1,Math.min(s.maxCols-s.widget.x,s.startW+dx));

    const nh=s.startH-dy;const ny=s.startY+dy;

    if(nh>=1&&ny>=0){s.widget.h=nh;s.widget.y=ny;}

  }else if(s.mode==='resize-sw'){

    const nw=s.startW-dx;const nx=s.startX+dx;

    if(nw>=1&&nx>=0){s.widget.w=nw;s.widget.x=nx;}

    s.widget.h=Math.max(1,Math.min(s.maxRows-s.widget.y,s.startH+dy));

  }else if(s.mode==='resize-nw'){

    const nw=s.startW-dx;const nx=s.startX+dx;

    if(nw>=1&&nx>=0){s.widget.w=nw;s.widget.x=nx;}

    const nh=s.startH-dy;const ny=s.startY+dy;

    if(nh>=1&&ny>=0){s.widget.h=nh;s.widget.y=ny;}

  }

  renderDesignerCanvas();

}

function endDrag(){

  document.removeEventListener('mousemove',onDrag);

  document.removeEventListener('mouseup',endDrag);

  _dragState=null;

  renderPropsPanel();

  try{persist();}catch(e){}

}

 

function addWidgetToCanvas(type,x,y){

  const w=WIDGETS[type];if(!w)return;

  const id=type+'_'+Date.now();

  const widget={

    id,type,

    x:x??0,y:y??0,

    w:w.w,h:w.h,

    bg:'#ffffff06',border:1,opacity:100,fontScale:1,

    minCard:180,speed:30

  };

  // Sığmıyorsa sola sığdır

  if(widget.x+widget.w>S.canvas.gridCols)widget.x=S.canvas.gridCols-widget.w;

  if(widget.y+widget.h>S.canvas.gridRows)widget.y=S.canvas.gridRows-widget.h;

  if(widget.x<0)widget.x=0;if(widget.y<0)widget.y=0;

  if(!S.canvas.layout)S.canvas.layout=[];

  S.canvas.layout.push(widget);

  _selectedWidget=id;

  renderDesignerCanvas();

  renderPropsPanel();

  try{persist();}catch(e){}

  toast(`${w.name} eklendi`);

}

function deleteWidget(id){

  S.canvas.layout=S.canvas.layout.filter(w=>w.id!==id);

  _selectedWidget=null;

  renderDesignerCanvas();

  renderPropsPanel();

  try{persist();}catch(e){}

}

function toggleGrid(){_showGrid=!_showGrid;renderDesignerCanvas();}

function clearCanvas(){if(!confirm('Tüm widget\'lar silinsin mi?'))return;S.canvas.layout=[];_selectedWidget=null;renderDesignerCanvas();renderPropsPanel();}

function loadTemplate(name){

  if(!confirm('Mevcut düzen silinecek, devam?'))return;

  if(name==='default')S.canvas.layout=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_LAYOUT));

  else if(name==='pdf-focus')S.canvas.layout=[

    {id:'logo1',type:'logo',x:0,y:0,w:3,h:1,bg:'transparent',border:0,opacity:100},

    {id:'voc1',type:'voc',x:3,y:0,w:2,h:2,bg:'#ffffff06',border:1,opacity:100,fontScale:1},

    {id:'clock1',type:'clock',x:0,y:1,w:3,h:1,bg:'transparent',border:0,opacity:100,fontScale:1},

    {id:'rings1',type:'rings',x:0,y:2,w:5,h:2,bg:'transparent',border:0,opacity:100},

    {id:'dan1',type:'danismanlar',x:0,y:4,w:5,h:2,bg:'transparent',border:0,opacity:100,minCard:160},

    {id:'pdf1',type:'pdf',x:5,y:0,w:3,h:6,bg:'#ffffff06',border:1,opacity:100,speed:25},

  ];

  else if(name==='minimal')S.canvas.layout=[

    {id:'logo1',type:'logo',x:0,y:0,w:4,h:1,bg:'transparent',border:0,opacity:100},

    {id:'voc1',type:'voc',x:6,y:0,w:2,h:2,bg:'#ffffff06',border:1,opacity:100,fontScale:1.2},

    {id:'rings1',type:'rings',x:0,y:2,w:8,h:2,bg:'transparent',border:0,opacity:100},

    {id:'dan1',type:'danismanlar',x:0,y:4,w:8,h:2,bg:'transparent',border:0,opacity:100,minCard:200},

  ];

  _selectedWidget=null;

  renderDesignerCanvas();

  renderPropsPanel();

  try{persist();}catch(e){}

  toast('Şablon yüklendi');

}

 

function renderPropsPanel(){

  const c=$('designer-props');if(!c)return;

  const w=(S.canvas.layout||[]).find(x=>x.id===_selectedWidget);

  if(!w){c.innerHTML='<h3>Özellikler</h3><div class="prop-empty">Düzenlemek için bir widget seçin</div>';return;}

  const def=WIDGETS[w.type];

  let html=`<h3>${def?def.icon:''} ${def?def.name:w.type}</h3>`;

  html+=`<div class="prop-row"><label>Pozisyon (X / Y)</label><div class="prop-pair"><input type="number" id="pp-x" value="${w.x}" min="0" max="${S.canvas.gridCols-1}"><input type="number" id="pp-y" value="${w.y}" min="0" max="${S.canvas.gridRows-1}"></div></div>`;

  html+=`<div class="prop-row"><label>Boyut (W / H)</label><div class="prop-pair"><input type="number" id="pp-w" value="${w.w}" min="1" max="${S.canvas.gridCols}"><input type="number" id="pp-h" value="${w.h}" min="1" max="${S.canvas.gridRows}"></div></div>`;

  html+=`<div class="prop-row"><label>Arka Plan Rengi</label><input type="text" id="pp-bg" value="${w.bg||'transparent'}" placeholder="transparent veya #ffffff06"></div>`;

  html+=`<div class="prop-row"><label>Şeffaflık: <span style="color:var(--accent);font-weight:700" id="pp-op-lbl">${w.opacity??100}</span>%</label><input type="range" id="pp-op" min="0" max="100" value="${w.opacity??100}"></div>`;

  html+=`<div class="prop-row"><label>Kenarlık</label><select id="pp-border"><option value="0" ${!w.border?'selected':''}>Yok</option><option value="1" ${w.border?'selected':''}>Var</option></select></div>`;

  if(def&&def.props.includes('fontScale'))html+=`<div class="prop-row"><label>Font Ölçek: <span style="color:var(--accent)" id="pp-fs-lbl">${w.fontScale||1}</span>x</label><input type="range" id="pp-fs" min="0.5" max="2" step="0.1" value="${w.fontScale||1}"></div>`;

  if(def&&def.props.includes('minCard'))html+=`<div class="prop-row"><label>Kart Min Genişlik: <span style="color:var(--accent)" id="pp-mc-lbl">${w.minCard||180}</span>px</label><input type="range" id="pp-mc" min="120" max="300" step="10" value="${w.minCard||180}"></div>`;

  if(def&&def.props.includes('speed'))html+=`<div class="prop-row"><label>Kayma Hızı: <span style="color:var(--accent)" id="pp-sp-lbl">${w.speed||30}</span>s</label><input type="range" id="pp-sp" min="10" max="120" value="${w.speed||30}"></div>`;

  if(def&&def.props.includes('commentCount'))html+=`<div class="prop-row"><label>Yorum Sayısı: <span style="color:var(--accent)" id="pp-cc-lbl">${w.commentCount||10}</span></label><input type="range" id="pp-cc" min="1" max="50" value="${w.commentCount||10}"></div>`;

  html+=`<div class="prop-row" style="margin-top:16px"><button class="btn btn-red btn-sm" style="width:100%" onclick="deleteWidget('${w.id}')">🗑 Bu Widget'ı Sil</button></div>`;

  c.innerHTML=html;

 

  // Event listeners

  const onProp=()=>{

    w.x=+$('pp-x').value;w.y=+$('pp-y').value;

    w.w=+$('pp-w').value;w.h=+$('pp-h').value;

    w.bg=$('pp-bg').value;

    w.opacity=+$('pp-op').value;$('pp-op-lbl').textContent=w.opacity;

    w.border=+$('pp-border').value;

    if($('pp-fs')){w.fontScale=+$('pp-fs').value;$('pp-fs-lbl').textContent=w.fontScale;}

    if($('pp-mc')){w.minCard=+$('pp-mc').value;$('pp-mc-lbl').textContent=w.minCard;}

    if($('pp-sp')){w.speed=+$('pp-sp').value;$('pp-sp-lbl').textContent=w.speed;}

    if($('pp-cc')){w.commentCount=+$('pp-cc').value;$('pp-cc-lbl').textContent=w.commentCount;}

    renderDesignerCanvas();

    // Anında localStorage'a yaz - Kiosk doğru veriyi alsın

    try{persist();}catch(e){}

  };

  ['pp-x','pp-y','pp-w','pp-h','pp-bg','pp-op','pp-border','pp-fs','pp-mc','pp-sp'].forEach(id=>{const el=$(id);if(el){el.oninput=onProp;el.onchange=onProp;}});

}

 

// =================================================================

// WIDGET HTML RENDERER (canvas + kiosk ortak)

// =================================================================

function renderWidgetHtml(w,preview){

  switch(w.type){

    case 'voc':{

      const score=S.voc.score||0;

      const displayScore=Number.isInteger(score)?score:score.toFixed(1);

      const anketDelta=S._newAnketDelta||0;

      const deltaHtml=anketDelta>0?`<div class="w-voc-delta" style="font-size:clamp(10px,1.5cqw,14px);color:#22c55e;font-weight:700;margin-top:4px;animation:fadeInUp 0.6s ease">📊 +${anketDelta} yeni anket</div>`:'';

      // Aylık VOC (dönem VOC'undan farklıysa göster)

      const mVoc=S._monthVoc;

      const mVocHtml=(mVoc!==null&&mVoc!==undefined)?`<div style="font-size:clamp(10px,1.5cqw,14px);color:var(--accent);font-weight:700;margin-top:3px;opacity:0.85">📅 Ay VOC: %${Number.isInteger(mVoc)?mVoc:mVoc.toFixed(1)}</div>`:'';

      const vocWrapAttrs=preview?'':` style="cursor:pointer" onclick="event.stopPropagation();toggleInlinePicker('voc',this)" title="Tıkla: dönem/ay değiştir (sadece bu cihaz)"`;

      const vocBadge='';

      const vocSubHtml=`<div class="w-voc-sub">${S.currentShowing||''}</div>`;

      if(S._noData){

        return `<div class="w-voc"${vocWrapAttrs}><div class="w-voc-lbl">VOC ENDEKS</div><div class="w-voc-val" style="font-size:calc(clamp(20px,4cqw,48px)*${w.fontScale||1});color:var(--muted)">Veri Yok</div>${vocSubHtml}${vocBadge}</div>`;

      }

      const ayAnket=S.anket.ay||0;

      const donemAnket=S.anket.toplam||0;

      const ayAnketHtml=(mVoc!==null&&mVoc!==undefined)?`<div style="font-size:clamp(9px,1.1cqw,12px);color:var(--muted);margin-top:1px">📆 Bu ay: ${ayAnket} anket</div>`:'';

      const donemAnketHtml=`<div style="font-size:clamp(9px,1.1cqw,12px);color:var(--muted);margin-top:1px">🗓️ Bu dönem: ${donemAnket} anket</div>`;

      return `<div class="w-voc"${vocWrapAttrs}><div class="w-voc-lbl">VOC ENDEKS</div><div class="w-voc-val" style="font-size:calc(clamp(28px,7cqw,80px)*${w.fontScale||1})">%${displayScore}</div>${vocSubHtml}${mVocHtml}<div class="w-voc-anket" style="font-size:clamp(10px,1.3cqw,13px);color:var(--muted);margin-top:2px">📋 ${(S.vocRawData||[]).length} anket (tüm zamanlar)</div>${ayAnketHtml}${donemAnketHtml}${deltaHtml}${vocBadge}</div>`;

    }

    case 'logo':{

      const hasLogo=!!S.brand.logo;

      return `<div class="w-logo"><div class="w-logo-box ${hasLogo?'has-image':''}">${hasLogo?`<img src="${S.brand.logo}">`:`<span>${(S.brand.name[0]||'E').toUpperCase()}</span>`}</div><div class="w-logo-text"><div class="w-logo-name">${S.brand.name||'—'}</div><div class="w-logo-sub">${S.brand.sub||''}</div>${S.currentShowing&&S.currentShowing!=='—'?`<div class="w-logo-badge">${S.currentShowing}</div>`:''}</div></div>`;

    }

    case 'clock':{

      const n=new Date();

      const d=n.toLocaleDateString('tr-TR',{weekday:'long',day:'numeric',month:'long'});

      const t=n.toLocaleTimeString('tr-TR',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});

      return `<div class="w-clock"><div class="w-clock-date">${d}</div><div class="w-clock-time" style="font-size:calc(clamp(24px,5cqw,56px)*${w.fontScale||1})">${t}</div></div>`;

    }

    case 'rings':{

      if(S._noData){

        return `<div class="w-rings" style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center"><div style="text-align:center;color:var(--muted);font-size:clamp(14px,2cqw,22px);font-weight:600;padding:20px">📭 Bu dönemde veri yok</div></div>`;

      }

      const P=2*Math.PI*42;

      // Eşik bilgisi

      const thresholds=(S._thresholds||[]);

      const cards=(S.metrics||[]).map((m,mi)=>{

        const val=m.value||0;

        const displayVal=Number.isInteger(val)?val:val.toFixed(1);

        const off=(P*(1-val/100)).toFixed(1);

        // Eşik kontrolü: dinamik (Sheets'ten) veya fallback %80

        const thr=thresholds[mi]!==undefined?thresholds[mi]:80;

        const thrColor=val>=thr?'#22c55e':'#ef4444';

        const borderStyle=`border:2px solid ${thrColor};box-shadow:0 0 8px ${thrColor}44`;

        return `<div class="w-ring-card" style="${borderStyle}" onclick="openRingModal(${mi})" data-ring-idx="${mi}"><svg class="w-ring-svg" viewBox="0 0 100 100"><circle cx="50" cy="50" r="42" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.08)" stroke-width="6" fill="none"/><circle cx="50" cy="50" r="42" stroke="${m.color}" stroke-width="6" fill="none" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-dasharray="${P.toFixed(1)}" stroke-dashoffset="${off}" style="transform:rotate(-90deg);transform-origin:50px 50px;transition:stroke-dashoffset 1.6s cubic-bezier(0.4,0,0.2,1);filter:drop-shadow(0 0 6px ${m.color})"/><text x="50" y="48" text-anchor="middle" dominant-baseline="middle" font-family="Space Grotesk,sans-serif" font-weight="800" font-size="22" fill="currentColor">%${displayVal}</text><text x="50" y="64" text-anchor="middle" font-size="7" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.45)">PUAN</text></svg><div class="w-ring-lbl">${m.name}</div></div>`;

      }).join('');

      return `<div class="w-rings">${cards}</div>`;

    }

    case 'danismanlar':{

      if(S._noData){

        return `<div class="w-dan"><div class="w-dan-title">Danışman Performansı</div><div style="flex:1;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;color:var(--muted);font-size:clamp(12px,1.4cqw,16px)">📭 Bu dönemde veri yok</div></div>`;

      }

      const minC=w.minCard||180;

      const danThr=(S._thresholds||[])[0]||80; // Genel eşik

      const cards=(S.danismanlar||[]).map(d=>{const v=d.voc||0;const displayV=Number.isInteger(v)?v:v.toFixed(1);const borderColor=v>=danThr?'#22c55e':'#ef4444';const anonIcon=d.isAnonim?'🕵️ ':'';const av=d.photo?`<img src="${d.photo}">`:(d.isAnonim?'🕵️':inits(d.name));const safeName=d.name.replace(/'/g,"\\'");return `<div class="w-dan-card" style="--dan-color:${d.color};border:2px solid ${borderColor};box-shadow:0 0 6px ${borderColor}33;cursor:pointer" onclick="openDanModal('${safeName}')"><div class="w-dan-av">${av}</div><div class="w-dan-info"><div class="w-dan-name">${anonIcon}${d.name}</div><div class="w-dan-score"><strong>%${displayV}</strong>${d.anket?` · ${d.anket} anket`:''}${d.geriDonus?`<span style="color:#ef4444;font-size:10px;font-weight:700"> · ↩${d.geriDonus}</span>`:''}</div><div class="w-dan-bar-bg"><div class="w-dan-bar" style="width:${v}%"></div></div></div></div>`;}).join('');

      return `<div class="w-dan"><div class="w-dan-title">Danışman Performansı</div><div class="w-dan-list" style="grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(${minC}px,1fr))">${cards||'<div style="color:var(--muted);font-size:11px;padding:8px">Danışman yok</div>'}</div></div>`;

    }

    case 'stats':{

      const stats=[

        {l:'Toplam Anket (tüm zamanlar)',v:(S.vocRawData||[]).length,onclick:"openAnketListModal()",style:"cursor:pointer"},

        {l:'TR Ortalama',v:'%'+(S.voc.tr||0),onclick:"",style:""},

        {l:'1. Hedef',v:'%'+(S.voc.target1||0),onclick:"",style:""},

        {l:'Geri Dönüş',v:'%'+(S.anket.geri||0),onclick:"openGeriDonusModal()",style:"cursor:pointer"}

      ];

      const cards=stats.map(st=>`<div class="w-stat" style="${st.style}" onclick="${st.onclick}"><div class="w-stat-val">${st.v}</div><div class="w-stat-lbl">${st.l}${st.onclick?' 🔍':''}</div></div>`).join('');

      return `<div class="w-stats"><div class="w-stats-title">Özet</div><div class="w-stats-grid">${cards}</div></div>`;

    }

    case 'trend':{

      const arr=S.trend.filter(t=>t.value>0);

      const data=arr.length?arr:S.trend;

      const max=Math.max(...data.map(t=>t.value),100);

      const cols=data.map(t=>{const h=(t.value/max)*100;return `<div class="w-trend-col"><div class="w-trend-bar" style="height:${h}%;background:${t.cur?'var(--accent)':'var(--border-strong)'}"></div><div class="w-trend-lbl">${t.label}</div></div>`;}).join('');

      return `<div class="w-trend"><div class="w-trend-title">Aylık Trend</div><div class="w-trend-chart">${cols}</div></div>`;

    }

    case 'egitim':{

      const rows=(S.egitim.items||[]).map(it=>`<div class="w-egitim-row"><div class="w-egitim-ico">🎓</div><div class="w-egitim-lbl">${it.label||'—'}</div><div class="w-egitim-val">${it.value||'—'}</div></div>`).join('');

      return `<div class="w-egitim"><div class="w-egitim-title">${S.egitim.title||'🎓 Eğitim'}</div><div class="w-egitim-list">${rows||'<div style="color:var(--muted);font-size:11px;padding:8px">Henüz satır eklenmedi</div>'}</div></div>`;

    }

    case 'calls48h':{

      const pick=pick48hForDisplay();

      const cards=pick.items.length?pick.items.map(it=>{

        const trimmed=smartTrim(it.aciklama,120);

        const moodIcon=it.mood==='kirmizi'?'⚠️':(it.mood==='yesil'?'✓':(it.mood==='turuncu'?'!':''));

        const moodBadgeLabel=it.mood==='kirmizi'?'NEGATİF YORUM':(it.mood==='yesil'?'POZİTİF YORUM':(it.mood==='turuncu'?'TAKİPTE':it.moodLabel));

        const plakaHtml=it.plaka?`<span class="w-48h-plaka">${it.plaka}</span>`:'';

        const yanitBadge=it.yanit?`<span style="background:${it.yanitDurum==='OLUMLU'?'#22c55e':(it.yanitDurum==='OLUMSUZ'?'#ef4444':'#888')};color:#000;font-size:9px;font-weight:800;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:99px;margin-left:4px">✎</span>`:'';

        const realIdx=(pick.day&&S.calls48h.data[pick.day])?S.calls48h.data[pick.day].indexOf(it):'';

        return `<div class="w-48h-card" data-mood="${it.mood}" style="--mood-color:${it.moodColor};--mood-bg:${it.moodColor}22;cursor:pointer" onclick="open48hModal(this)" data-full-aciklama="${(it.aciklama||'').replace(/"/g,'&quot;')}" data-danisman="${(it.danisman||'').replace(/"/g,'&quot;')}" data-musteri="${(it.musteri||'').replace(/"/g,'&quot;')}" data-plaka="${(it.plaka||'').replace(/"/g,'&quot;')}" data-is="${(it.is||'').replace(/"/g,'&quot;')}" data-skf="${(it.skfNo||'').replace(/"/g,'&quot;')}" data-mood-label="${it.moodLabel||''}" data-mood-color="${it.moodColor}" data-sheetname="${it.sheetName||''}" data-rowindex="${it.rowIndex||''}" data-day="${pick.day||''}" data-idx="${realIdx}" data-yanit="${(it.yanit||'').replace(/"/g,'&quot;')}" data-yanitdurum="${it.yanitDurum||''}"><div class="w-48h-card-head"><span class="w-48h-mood-icon" style="color:${it.moodColor}">${moodIcon}</span><span class="w-48h-who">${it.danisman||'—'}</span>${plakaHtml}<span class="w-48h-mood" style="background:${it.moodColor}">${moodBadgeLabel}</span>${yanitBadge}</div><div class="w-48h-txt">${trimmed||'<i>(yorum yok)</i>'}</div>${it.musteri?`<div class="w-48h-meta">${it.musteri}</div>`:''}</div>`;

      }).join(''):'';

      const inner=cards?((preview||_renderMobileMode)?cards:cards+cards):'<div style="padding:20px;text-align:center;color:var(--muted);font-size:11px">Veri yok</div>';

      const headAttrs48=preview?'':` style="cursor:pointer" onclick="openWidgetFullscreen('48h')" title="Tıkla: tümünü aç ve gün seç"`;

      const badge48='';

      return `<div class="w-48h"><div class="w-48h-head"${headAttrs48}><div class="w-48h-ico">📞</div><div class="w-48h-title">48 Saat Aramaları</div>${badge48}<div class="w-48h-date">${pick.dateStr||''}</div></div><div class="w-48h-track"><div class="w-48h-inner" data-speed="${w.speed||30}">${inner}</div></div></div>`;

    }

    case 'pdf':{

      return `<div class="w-pdf" onclick="${preview?'':'openPdfFullscreen()'}"><div class="w-pdf-head"><div class="w-pdf-title" id="pdf-title-${w.id}">PDF</div><div class="w-pdf-badge">📄 TIKLA</div></div><div class="w-pdf-content"><div class="w-pdf-inner" id="pdf-inner-${w.id}" data-speed="${w.speed||25}"><div style="padding:20px;text-align:center;color:var(--muted);font-size:11px">${preview?'PDF önizleme':'Yükleniyor...'}</div></div></div></div>`;

    }

    case 'ticker':{

      const items=S.ticker.items&&S.ticker.items.length?S.ticker.items:['Veri bekleniyor...'];

      const sep='<span style="color:rgba(255,255,255,0.3);margin:0 18px"> ◆ </span>';

      const html=items.map(t=>`<span style="color:var(--text);font-weight:600;font-size:clamp(12px,1.4cqw,16px);white-space:nowrap">${t}</span>`).join(sep);

      const dur=Math.round(65-(w.speed||30));

      const tickerInner=_renderMobileMode?html:`${html}${sep}${html}`;

      return `<div class="w-ticker"><div class="w-ticker-badge"><span class="w-ticker-dot"></span>CANLI</div><div class="w-ticker-track"><div class="w-ticker-inner" style="animation-duration:${dur}s">${tickerInner}</div></div></div>`;

    }

    case 'comments':{

      const count=w.commentCount||10;

      const rawData=S.vocRawData||[];

      const commentsLocal=!preview&&LV.comments&&LV.comments.active&&LV.comments.date;

      let withComments;

      let commentsLabel;

      if(commentsLocal){

        // Bu cihaza özel: seçilen güne ait TÜM anketler (yorumlu + sadece puanlamalı) — nihai sonuç

        withComments=rawData.filter(r=>r.tarih&&localISO(r.tarih)===LV.comments.date).sort((a,b)=>b.tarih-a.tarih);

        commentsLabel=new Date(LV.comments.date+'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString('tr-TR',{day:'2-digit',month:'short'})+' 📍';

      }else if(_renderMobileMode){

        // Mobil varsayılan: sadece verinin olduğu EN SON GÜNE ait tüm anketler (yorumlu+yorumsuz).

        // Daha fazlasını görmek isterse widget'a basıp tam ekran takvimden tarih seçebilir.

        const withDate=rawData.filter(r=>r.tarih);

        if(withDate.length){

          const latestDate=withDate.reduce((a,b)=>a.tarih>b.tarih?a:b).tarih;

          const latestISO=localISO(latestDate);

          withComments=withDate.filter(r=>localISO(r.tarih)===latestISO).sort((a,b)=>b.tarih-a.tarih);

          commentsLabel=latestDate.toLocaleDateString('tr-TR',{day:'2-digit',month:'short'});

        }else{

          withComments=[];commentsLabel='Son 0';

        }

      }else{

        // Yayındaki (masaüstü): TÜM anketler (yorumlu + sadece puanlamalı) tarihe göre sırala, son N tanesi

        withComments=rawData.filter(r=>r.tarih).sort((a,b)=>b.tarih-a.tarih).slice(0,count);

        commentsLabel='Son '+withComments.length;

      }

      const headAttrsC=preview?'':` style="cursor:pointer" onclick="openWidgetFullscreen('comments')" title="Tıkla: tümünü aç ve tarih seç"`;

      const badgeC='';

      const dateHtmlComments=`<div class="w-48h-date">${commentsLabel}</div>`;

      if(!withComments.length){

        return `<div class="w-48h"><div class="w-48h-head"${headAttrsC}><div class="w-48h-ico">💬</div><div class="w-48h-title">Müşteri Yorumları</div>${badgeC}${dateHtmlComments}</div><div class="w-48h-track"><div style="padding:20px;text-align:center;color:var(--muted);font-size:11px">${commentsLocal?'Bu tarihte anket yok':'Yorum bulunamadı'}</div></div></div>`;

      }

      const cards=withComments.map(r=>{

        // Çoğunluk puana göre renk: 5→yeşil, 4→turuncu, 1-3→kırmızı

        const puanlar=[r.q1,r.q2,r.q3,r.q4,r.q5].filter(v=>v>=1&&v<=5);

        const c5=puanlar.filter(v=>v===5).length;

        const cNeg=puanlar.filter(v=>v<=3).length;

        const c4=puanlar.filter(v=>v===4).length;

        let moodColor='#4ecdc4'; // default nötr

        if(c5>c4&&c5>cNeg)moodColor='#22c55e'; // yeşil

        else if(cNeg>c5)moodColor='#ef4444'; // kırmızı

        else if(c4>=c5)moodColor='#f59e0b'; // turuncu

        const trimmed=(r.yorum&&r.yorum.length>3)?smartTrim(r.yorum,120):'<i style="opacity:.55">Yazılı yorum yok — sadece puanlama</i>';

        const icon=r.danisman==='ANONİM'?'🕵️ ':'';

        // Küçük puan gösterimi — sadece dolu puanları göster

        const puanHtml=puanlar.map((v,i)=>{

          const clr=v===5?'#22c55e':(v===4?'#f59e0b':'#ef4444');

          return `<span style="color:${clr};font-weight:700;font-size:10px">${v}</span>`;

        }).join('<span style="color:rgba(255,255,255,0.2);margin:0 2px">·</span>');

        const musteriData=JSON.stringify({danisman:r.danisman,musteriAd:r.musteriAd,musteriSoyad:r.musteriSoyad,aracModel:r.aracModel,sase:r.sase,q1:r.q1,q2:r.q2,q3:r.q3,q4:r.q4,q5:r.q5,yorum:r.yorum,tarih:r.tarih}).replace(/"/g,'&quot;');

        const tarihKisa=r.tarih?r.tarih.toLocaleDateString('tr-TR',{day:'numeric',month:'short'}):'';

        return `<div class="w-48h-card" style="--mood-color:${moodColor};--mood-bg:${moodColor}22;cursor:pointer" onclick="openCommentModal(this)" data-comment='${musteriData}'><div class="w-48h-card-head"><span class="w-48h-who">${icon}${r.danisman}</span><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px">${puanHtml}<span style="color:rgba(255,255,255,0.3)">·</span><span style="font-size:9px;opacity:0.6">${tarihKisa}</span></span></div><div class="w-48h-txt">${trimmed}</div></div>`;

      }).join('');

      if(_renderMobileMode){

        return `<div class="w-48h"><div class="w-48h-head"${headAttrsC}><div class="w-48h-ico">💬</div><div class="w-48h-title">Müşteri Yorumları</div>${badgeC}${dateHtmlComments}</div><div class="w-48h-track" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px">${cards}</div></div>`;

      }

      const inner=preview?cards:cards+cards;

      return `<div class="w-48h"><div class="w-48h-head"${headAttrsC}><div class="w-48h-ico">💬</div><div class="w-48h-title">Müşteri Yorumları</div>${badgeC}${dateHtmlComments}</div><div class="w-48h-track"><div class="w-48h-inner" data-speed="${w.speed||30}">${inner}</div></div></div>`;

    }

    default: return `<div style="padding:8px;color:var(--muted);font-size:11px">${w.type}</div>`;

  }

}

 

// =================================================================

// KIOSK RENDER

// =================================================================

function stopKioskTimers(){if(_clockTimer){clearInterval(_clockTimer);_clockTimer=null;}if(_slideshowTimer){clearInterval(_slideshowTimer);_slideshowTimer=null;}if(_autoRefreshTimer){clearInterval(_autoRefreshTimer);_autoRefreshTimer=null;}}

let _autoRefreshTimer=null;

function applyKioskBg(){

  const bg=S.bg||{};const color=bg.color||'#06080f';

  document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--kiosk-bg',color);

  document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--kiosk-bg-rgb',hexToRgb(color));

  document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--bg-opacity',(bg.opacity||40)/100);

  if(bg.type==='solid'){$('k-bg-gradient').style.display='block';$('k-bg-video').style.display='none';$('k-bg-slideshow').style.display='none';const rgb=hexToRgb(color);$('k-bg-gradient').style.background=`radial-gradient(ellipse at 30% 20%,rgba(${rgb},1),${color} 60%)`;}

  else if(bg.type==='video'&&bg.video){$('k-bg-gradient').style.background=color;$('k-bg-video').style.display='block';$('k-bg-video').src=bg.video;$('k-bg-slideshow').style.display='none';}

  else if(bg.type==='image'&&bg.images&&bg.images.length){$('k-bg-gradient').style.background=color;$('k-bg-video').style.display='none';$('k-bg-slideshow').style.display='block';renderSlideshow();}

  else{$('k-bg-gradient').style.background=color;$('k-bg-video').style.display='none';$('k-bg-slideshow').style.display='none';}

}

function renderSlideshow(){const c=$('k-bg-slideshow');c.innerHTML='';if(_slideshowTimer)clearInterval(_slideshowTimer);if(!S.bg.images||!S.bg.images.length)return;S.bg.images.forEach((img,i)=>{const s=document.createElement('div');s.className='k-bg-slide';s.style.backgroundImage=`url(${img})`;if(i===0)s.classList.add('on');c.appendChild(s);});_slideIdx=0;if(S.bg.images.length>1)_slideshowTimer=setInterval(()=>{const slides=c.querySelectorAll('.k-bg-slide');slides.forEach(s=>s.classList.remove('on'));_slideIdx=(_slideIdx+1)%slides.length;slides[_slideIdx].classList.add('on');},(S.bg.slideDur||8)*1000);}

 

// Ekranda görünecek her şeyin hafif bir "özetini" çıkarır. Otomatik yenilemede

// bunu karşılaştırarak, veri gerçekten değişmediyse tüm ekranı (ve PDF'leri)

// sıfırdan yeniden çizmekten kaçınırız — böylece gereksiz titreme/sıçrama olmaz.

function computeKioskSignature(){

  try{

    const sig={

      canvas:(S.canvas&&S.canvas.layout)?S.canvas.layout.map(w=>`${w.id}:${w.x},${w.y},${w.w},${w.h},${w.bg},${w.opacity}`).join('|'):'',

      brand:S.brand?(S.brand.name+'|'+S.brand.sub+'|'+(S.brand.logo||'').length):'',

      bg:S.bg?(S.bg.type+'|'+S.bg.color+'|'+(S.bg.images||[]).length+'|'+(S.bg.video?1:0)):'',

      viewMode:JSON.stringify(S.viewMode),

      voc:S.voc?S.voc.score:'',

      metrics:(S.metrics||[]).map(m=>m.name+':'+m.value).join(','),

      anket:JSON.stringify(S.anket),

      danismanlar:(S.danismanlar||[]).map(d=>d.name+':'+d.voc+':'+d.anket).join(','),

      currentShowing:S.currentShowing,

      vocRawCount:(S.vocRawData||[]).length,

      calls48h:Object.keys((S.calls48h&&S.calls48h.data)||{}).map(d=>d+':'+(S.calls48h.data[d]||[]).length).join(','),

      pdfs:(S.pdfs||[]).map(p=>p.title+'|'+(p.url||'')+'|'+((p.data||'').length)).join(','),

      egitim:JSON.stringify(S.egitim),

      ticker:((S.ticker&&S.ticker.items)||[]).join('|'),

      LV:JSON.stringify(LV)

    };

    return JSON.stringify(sig);

  }catch(e){return 'sig-error-'+Date.now();} // hata olursa güvenli taraf: her zaman "değişti" say

}

let _lastKioskSig=null;

function renderKiosk(){

  document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme',S.theme);

  document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--accent',S.accent);

  document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--accent-rgb',hexToRgb(S.accent));

  applyKioskBg();

  // Önce YAYINDAKİ (published) görünümü baştan hesapla — bu, "otomatik/yayındaki"ye

  // dönüşte önceki yerel seçimden kalan değerlerin ekranda takılı kalmasını engeller.

  if(S.excelData&&Object.keys(S.excelData).length)applyViewMode();

  // Bu cihaza özel yerel görünüm varsa (yayını etkilemeden), en son burada uygula

  applyLocalVocOverride();

  closeInlinePicker();

 

  const canvas=$('k-canvas');canvas.innerHTML='';

  const isMobile=isMobileViewport();

  _lastKioskMobileMode=isMobile;

  _renderMobileMode=isMobile;

  const skioskEl=$('s-kiosk');

  if(skioskEl)skioskEl.classList.toggle('mobile-scroll',isMobile);

  document.body.classList.toggle('kiosk-mobile-lock',isMobile);

  if(isMobile){

    // ================= MOBİL: alt alta, tam genişlik, doğal akış =================

    canvas.className='k-canvas mobile-mode';

    canvas.style.width='';canvas.style.height='';canvas.style.position='';

    canvas.style.transform='';canvas.style.left='';canvas.style.top='';

    // Tasarımdaki üstten-alta / soldan-sağa okuma sırasına göre diz

    // Ticker (kayan yazı) mobilde hiç gösterilmiyor — anlamı yok, sayfa zaten kayıyor

    const sorted=[...(S.canvas.layout||[])].filter(w=>w.type!=='ticker').sort((a,b)=>(a.y-b.y)||(a.x-b.x));

    sorted.forEach(w=>{

      const el=document.createElement('div');

      el.className='k-widget';

      el.dataset.id=w.id;

      el.style.background=w.bg||'transparent';

      el.style.border=w.border?'1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.12)':'none';

      el.style.borderRadius='14px';

      el.style.opacity=(w.opacity??100)/100;

      el.style.backdropFilter=w.bg&&w.bg!=='transparent'?'blur(10px)':'';

      el.innerHTML=renderWidgetHtml(w,false);

      canvas.appendChild(el);

      if(w.type==='pdf')initPdfTape(w);

      // 48h/comments/ticker mobilde iç kaydırma animasyonu istemiyor — sayfa kendisi kayıyor zaten

    });

  }else{

    // ================= MASAÜSTÜ: 1920x1080 sabit tasarım, ölçekle sığdır (değişmedi) =================

    canvas.className='k-canvas';

    canvas.style.width=CANVAS_REAL.w+'px';

    canvas.style.height=CANVAS_REAL.h+'px';

    canvas.style.position='absolute';

    const vw=window.innerWidth,vh=window.innerHeight;

    const kScale=Math.min(vw/CANVAS_REAL.w,vh/CANVAS_REAL.h);

    canvas.style.transform=`scale(${kScale})`;

    canvas.style.transformOrigin='top left';

    canvas.style.left=Math.max(0,(vw-CANVAS_REAL.w*kScale)/2)+'px';

    canvas.style.top=Math.max(0,(vh-CANVAS_REAL.h*kScale)/2)+'px';

    const cols=S.canvas.gridCols,rows=S.canvas.gridRows;

    const cellW=CANVAS_REAL.w/cols,cellH=CANVAS_REAL.h/rows;

    (S.canvas.layout||[]).forEach(w=>{

      const el=document.createElement('div');

      el.className='k-widget';

      el.dataset.id=w.id;

      el.style.left=(w.x*cellW)+'px';

      el.style.top=(w.y*cellH)+'px';
